  "instructions": "Refine BranchesPage with Luma design tokens and scroll reveal animations",
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { api } from '../../services/api';\nimport Reveal from '../../components/ScrollReveal';\nimport { LoadingPage, PageHeader } from '../../components/ui/Elements';\n\nexport default function BranchesPage() {\n  const [branches, setBranches] = useState([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    api.get('/branches/').then(res => setBranches(res.data)).finally(() => setLoading(false));\n  }, []);\n\n  if (loading) return <LoadingPage />;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-12\">\n      <PageHeader\n        eyebrow=\"Find a location\"\n        title=\"Our Branches\"\n        subtitle=\"Find a branch near you. Check availability and book appointments.\"\n      />\n      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6\">\n        {branches.map((branch, i) => (\n          <Reveal key={branch.id} delay={(i % 3) * 90} direction=\"up\"\n            className=\"bg-surface-container-lowest border border-outline-variant/40 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm hover:shadow-md hover:-translate-y-0.5 transition-all\">\n            <div className=\"h-32 bg-gradient-to-br from-primary to-primary-container flex items-center justify-center relative overflow-hidden\">\n              <svg className=\"absolute inset-0 w-full h-full opacity-20\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 400 160\" preserveAspectRatio=\"none\">\n                <circle cx=\"320\" cy=\"20\" r=\"80\" stroke=\"#ffffff\" strokeWidth=\"2\" />\n                <circle cx=\"340\" cy=\"30\" r=\"50\" stroke=\"#ffffff\" strokeWidth=\"2\" />\n              </svg>\n              <div className=\"text-center text-white relative\">\n                <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-[28px] mb-1\">account_balance</span>\n                <div className=\"font-headline-md font-bold text-lg\">{branch.name}</div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"p-5 space-y-2\">\n              <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface flex items-start gap-2\">\n                <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-primary-container mt-0.5\">location_on</span>{branch.address}\n              </p>\n              <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant pl-6\">{branch.city}, {branch.state}</p>\n              <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant flex items-center gap-2 pl-6\">\n                <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-tertiary\">schedule</span>{branch.opening_hours}\n              </p>\n              <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant flex items-center gap-2 pl-6\">\n                <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-primary-container\">call</span>{branch.phone}\n              </p>\n            </div>\n          </Reveal>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

One connected experience.\"\n        subtitle=\"Great customer service, with zero waiting and zero confusion — that's what we're building.\"\n      />\n\n      <div className=\"space-y-8\">\n        <Reveal direction=\"right\" className=\"bg-surface-container-lowest border border-outline-variant/40 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm\">\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 mb-3\">\n            <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-primary-container text-[22px]\">visibility</span>\n            <h2 className=\"text-xl font-bold text-on-surface\">Our Vision</h2>\n          </div>\n          <p className=\"text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed\">\n            Luma is a connected service experience platform designed around the complete customer journey.\n            The core insight: customers experience problems as ONE journey, even though organizations often handle them as separate problems.\n          </p>\n        </Reveal>\n\n        <Reveal direction=\"left\" className=\"bg-surface-container-lowest border border-outline-variant/40 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm\">\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 mb-3\">\n            <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-error text-[22px]\">report_problem</span>\n            <h2 className=\"text-xl font-bold text-on-surface\">The Problem</h2>\n          </div>\n          <p className=\"text-on-surface-variant leading-relaxed mb-3\">\n            A customer needs a business account. They search for requirements, ask questions, visit a branch, get a queue ticket, wait,\n            discover missing documentation, leave frustrated, and complain online. Each step is handled in isolation.\n          </p>\n          <p className=\"text-on-surface font-semibold\">\n            Luma connects that entire journey into one seamless experience.\n          </p>\n        </Reveal>\n\n        <Reveal direction=\"up\" className=\"bg-surface-container-lowest border border-outline-variant/40 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm\">\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 mb-4\">\n            <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-primary-container text-[22px]\">grid_view</span>\n            <h2 className=\"text-xl font-bold text-on-surface\">Platform Modules</h2>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4\">\n            {[\n              { icon: 'confirmation_number', name:    { icon: 'hub', name: 'BranchConnect', desc: 'Internal branch knowledge sharing', bg: 'bg-primary-fixed/40', color: 'text-primary' },\n              { icon: 'insights', name: 'Luma Intelligence', desc: 'Executive analytics dashboard', bg: 'bg-tertiary-fixed/40', color: 'text-tertiary' },\n            ].map((m, i) => (\n              <Reveal key={m.name} delay={i * 70} className={`p-4 rounded-xl ${m.bg} flex items-start gap-3`}>\n                <span className={`material-symbols-outlined ${m.color} text-[22px]`}>{m.icon}</span>\n                <div>\n                  <h3 className=\"font-semibold text-on-surface\">{m.name}</h3>\n                  <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant\">{m.desc}</p>\n                </div>\n              </Reveal>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        </Reveal>\n\n        <Reveal direction=\"zoom\" className=\"text-center pt-4\">\n          <Link to=\"/services\" className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-2 h-12 px-8 rounded-lg bg-primary-container hover:bg-primary text-white text-base font-semibold transition-all shadow-[0_4px_14px_rgba(0,82,255,0.3)] hover:-translate-y-0.5\">\n            Get Started Free\n            <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]\">arrow_forward</span>\n          </Link>\n        </Reveal>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "path": "Frontend/src/pages/public/HelpPage.jsx",
  "instructions": "Refine HelpPage with Luma design tokens and scroll reveal animations",
  "content": "import Reveal from '../../components/ScrollReveal';\nimport { PageHeader, Alert } from '../../components/ui/Elements';\n\nexport default function HelpPage() {\n  const faqs = [\n    { q: 'How do I book an appointment?', a: 'Go to Services, select a service, choose a branch and date, then pick an available time slot.' },\n    { q: 'What documents do I need?', a: 'Each service has specific requirements listed. Check the service page before visiting a branch.' },\n    { q: 'How does TrustVerify work?', a: 'Upload your documents through the platform. Our AI analyzes them and tells you if they meet requirements before you visit the branch.' },\n    { q: 'What is SmartQueue?', a: 'SmartQueue lets you join a digital queue remotely. You get a ticket number, estimated wait time, and can track your position.' },\n    { q: 'How do I make a payment?', a: 'Go to Payments, select a service, and pay securely. Your payment is linked to your appointment and queue ticket.' },\n    { q: 'Can I give feedback?', a: 'Yes, go to Feedback and share your experience. Our SocialPulse AI analyzes sentiment and categorizes your feedback automatically.' },\n    { q: 'What is BranchConnect?', a: 'BranchConnect is an internal knowledge sharing platform where branches share solutions, lessons learned, and customer trends.' },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-12\">\n      <PageHeader\n        eyebrow=\"Support\"\n        title=\"Help Center\"\n        subtitle=\"Find answers to common questions about Luma.\"\n      />\n\n      <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n        {faqs.map((faq, i) => (\n          <Reveal key={i} delay={i * 60} direction=\"right\"\n            className=\"bg-surface-container-lowest border border-outline-variant/40 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm\">\n            <h3 className=\"font-semibold text-on-surface mb-2 flex items-center gap-2\">\n              <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-primary-container text-[18px]\">help</span>\n              {faq.q}\n            </h3>\n            <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant pl-7\">{faq.a}</p>\n          </Reveal>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n\n      <Reveal direction=\"zoom\" delay={200}\n        className=\"bg-surface-container rounded-2xl border border-outline-variant/40 p-6 mt-8 text-center shadow-sm\">\n        <h3 className=\"font-semibold text-on-surface mb-2\">Still need help?</h3>\n        <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant mb-3\">Contact our support team</p>\n        <p className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-primary font-data-mono\">hello@luma.com • +234 800 LUMA-HELP</p>\n      </Reveal>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

'../../components/ScrollReveal';\nimport { Field, Input, Alert } from '../../components/ui/Elements';\n\nexport default function LoginPage() {\n  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');\n  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');\n  const [error, setError] = useState('');\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);\n  const { login } = useAuth();\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n\n  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setError('');\n    setLoading(true);\n    try {\n      await login(email, password);\n      navigate('/dashboard');\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err.message || 'Login failed');\n    } finally {\n      setLoading(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const demoAccounts = [\n    { label: 'Customer', email: 'david@test.com' },\n    { label: 'Officer', email: 'officer1@wemaone.com' },\n    { label: 'Manager', email: 'manager1@wemaone.com' },\n    { label: 'Admin', email: 'admin@wemaone.com' },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-[85vh] flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-12 relative overflow-hidden\">\n      {/* Ambient backlight */}\n      <div className=\"absolute top-0 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-[600px] h-[300px] bg-gradient-to-tr from-primary/10 via-primary-container/10 to-transparent blur-3xl pointer-events-none -z-10\"></div>\n\n      <div className=\"w-full max-w-md\">\n        <Reveal direction=\"zoom\">\n          <div className=\"text-center mb-8\">\n            <div className=\"w-12 h-12 bg-primary-container rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 shadow-[0_4px_14px_rgba(0,82,255,0.3)]\">\n              <span className=\"text-white font-bold text-lg font-headline-md\">L</span>\n            </div>\n            <h1 className=\"font-headline-md text-2xl font-bold text-on-surface tracking-tight\">Welcome back</h1>\n            <p className=\"text-on-surface-variant text-sm mt-1\">Sign in to your Luma account</p>\n          </div>\n        </Reveal>\n\n        <Reveal direction=\"up\" delay={120} className=\"bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-2xl border border-outline-variant/40 p-6 shadow-xl\">\n          {error && <Alert kind=\"error\" className=\"mb-4\">{error}</Alert>}\n          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"space-y-4\">\n            <Field label=\"Email\">\n              <Input type=\"email\" value={email} onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)} required autoFocus />\n            </Field>\n            <Field label=\"Password\">\n              <Input type=\"password\" value={password} onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)} required />\n            </Field>\n            <button type=\"submit\" disabled={loading}\n              className=\"w-full bg-primary-container hover:bg-primary text-white py-2.5 rounded-lg font-semibold text-sm transition-all shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(0,82,255,0.25)] disabled:opacity-50 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2\">\n              {loading ? 'Signing in...' : 'Sign In'}\n              {!loading && <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]\">arrow_forward</span>}\n            </button>\n          </form>\n          <div className=\"mt-4 text-center\">\n            <Link to=\"/register\" className=\"text-sm text-primary hover:underline font-medium\">Create an account</Link>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-outline-variant/20\">\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-on-surface-variant text-center mb-2 font-data-mono-xs uppercase tracking-wider\">Demo accounts</p>\n            <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs\">\n              {demoAccounts.map(acc => (\n                <button key={acc.email} onClick={() => { setEmail(acc.email); setPassword('password123'); }}\n                  className=\"text-left p-2 bg-surface-container-low rounded-lg hover:bg-surface-container transition-colors text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface\">\n                  <span className=\"font-semibold text-primary\">{acc.label}:</span> {acc.email}\n                </button>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-on-surface-variant text-center mt-2 font-data-mono-xs\">Password: password123</p>\n          </div>\n        </Reveal>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "Frontend/src/pages/public/RegisterPage.jsx",
  "instructions": "Refine RegisterPage with Luma design tokens and scroll reveal animations",
  "content": "import { useState } from 'react';\nimport { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';\nimport Reveal from '../../components/ScrollReveal';\nimport { Field, Input, Alert } from '../../components/ui/Elements';\n\nexport default function RegisterPage() {\n  const [form, setForm] = useState({ first_name: '', last_name: '', email: '', phone: '', password: '' });\n  const [error, setError] = useState('');\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);\n  const { register } = useAuth();\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n\n  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setError('');\n    setLoading(true);\n    try {\n      await register(form);\n      navigate('/dashboard');\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err.message || 'Registration failed');\n    } finally {\n      setLoading(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const update = (field, value) => setForm(prev => ({ ...prev, [field]: value }));\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-[85vh] flex items-center justify-center px-4 py-12 relative overflow-hidden\">\n      {/* Ambient backlight */}\n      <div className=\"absolute top-0 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-[600px] h-[300px] bg-gradient-to-tr from-primary/10 via-primary-container/10 to-transparent blur-3xl pointer-events-none -z-10\"></div>\n\n      <div className=\"w-full max-w-md\">\n        <Reveal direction=\"zoom\">\n          <div className=\"text-center mb-8\">\n            <div className=\"w-12 h-12 bg-primary-container rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 shadow-[0_4px_14px_rgba(0,82,255,0.3)]\">\n              <span className=\"text-white font-bold text-lg font-headline-md\">L</span>\n            </div>\n            <h1 className=\"font-headline-md text-2xl font-bold text-on-surface tracking-tight\">Create your account</h1>\n            <p className=\"text-on-surface-variant text-sm mt-1\">Join Luma for a connected service experience</p>\n          </div>\n        </Reveal>\n\n        <Reveal direction=\"up\" delay={120} className=\"bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-2xl border border-outline-variant/40 p-6 shadow-xl\">\n          {error && <Alert kind=\"error\" className=\"mb-4\">{error}</Alert>}\n          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"space-y-4\">\n            <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-3\">\n              <Field label=\"First Name\">\n                <Input type=\"text\" value={form.first_name} onChange={e => update('first_name', e.target.value)} required />\n              </Field>\n              <Field label=\"Last Name\">\n                <Input type=\"text\" value={form.last_name} onChange={e => update('last_name', e.target.value)} required />\n              </Field>\n            </div>\n            <Field label=\"Email\">\n              <Input type=\"email\" value={form.email} onChange={e => update('email', e.target.value)} required />\n            </Field>\n            <Field label=\"Phone\">\n              <Input type=\"tel\" value={form.phone} onChange={e => update('phone', e.target.value)} />\n            </Field>\n            <Field label=\"Password\">\n              <Input type=\"password\" value={form.password} onChange={e => update('password', e.target.value)} required minLength={6} />\n            </Field>\n            <button type=\"submit\" disabled={loading}\n              className=\"w-full bg-primary-container hover:bg-primary text-white py-2.5 rounded-lg font-semibold text-sm transition-all shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(0,82,255,0.25)] disabled:opacity-50 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2\">\n              {loading ? 'Creating account...' : 'Create Account'}\n              {!loading && <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]\">arrow_forward</span>}\n            </button>\n          </form>\n          <div className=\"mt-4 text-center\">\n            <Link to=\"/login\" className=\"text-sm text-primary hover:underline font-medium\">Already have an account? Sign in</Link>\n          </div>\n        </Reveal>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "Frontend/src/pages/customer/CustomerDashboard.jsx",
  "instructions": "Refine CustomerDashboard with Luma design tokens and scroll reveal animations",
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { Link } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { api } from '../../services/api';\nimport { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';\nimport Reveal from '../../components/ScrollReveal';\nimport { StatusBadge, LoadingPage, Card, CardTitle } from '../../components/ui/Elements';\n\nexport default function CustomerDashboard() {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState([]);\n  const [tickets, setTickets] = useState([]);\n  const [feedbacks, setFeedbacks] = useState([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    Promise.all([\n      api.get('/appointments/'),\n      api.get('/queues/my-tickets'),\n      api.get('/feedback/my-feedback')\n    ]).then(([a, t, f]) => {\n      setAppointments(a.data);\n      setTickets(t.data);\n      setFeedbacks(f.data);\n    }).finally(() => setLoading(false));\n  }, []);\n\n  if (loading) return <LoadingPage />;\n\n  const activeAppts = appointments.filter(a => a.status === 'SCHEDULED' || a.status === 'CONFIRMED');\n  const activeTickets = tickets.filter(t => ['WAITING', 'CALLED', 'IN_SERVICE', 'CHECKED_IN'].includes(t.status));\n\n  const quickActions = [\n    { to: '/services', icon: 'search', label: 'Discover Services', bg: 'bg-primary-fixed/40', color: 'text-primary' },\n    { to: '/customer/queue', icon: 'confirmation_number', label: 'Join Queue', bg: 'bg-primary-fixed/40', color: 'text-primary' },\n    { to: '/customer/documents', icon: 'upload_file', label: 'Upload Docs', bg: 'bg-tertiary-fixed/40', color: 'text-tertiary' },\n    { to: '/customer/payments', icon: 'payments', label: 'Payments', bg: 'bg-tertiary-fixed/40', color: 'text-tertiary' },\n    { to: '/customer/feedback', icon: 'forum', label: 'Give Feedback', bg: 'bg-primary-fixed/40', color: 'text-primary' },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8\">\n      <Reveal direction=\"down\" className=\"mb-8 flex items-center justify-between gap-4\">\n        <div>\n          <span className=\"font-data-mono-xs text-xs uppercase text-primary font-semibold tracking-wider\">Your dashboard</span>\n          <h1 className=\"font-headline-md text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold text-on-surface tracking-tight\">Welcome, {user?.first_name}</h1>\n          <p className=\"text-on-surface-variant text-sm\">Everything for your visit, in one place.</p>\n        </div>\n        <span className=\"hidden sm:inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-tertiary-fixed/40 text-tertiary font-data-mono-xs text-xs font-semibold\">\n          <span className=\"w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-tertiary-container animate-pulse\"></span> All systems ready\n        </span>\n      </Reveal>\n\n      {/* Quick Actions */}\n      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4 mb-8\">\n        {quickActions.map((action, i) => (\n          <Reveal key={action.to} delay={i * 70}>\n            <Link to={action.to} className=\"block bg-surface-container-lowest border border-outline-variant/40 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md hover:-translate-y-0.5 transition-all text-center\">\n              <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl ${action.bg} flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-2`}>\n                <span className={`material-symbols-outlined ${action.color} text-[20px]`}>{action.icon}</span>\n              </div>\n              <span className=\"text-sm font-medium text-on-surface\">{action.label}</span>\n            </Link>\n          </Reveal>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6\">\n        {/* Active Queue */}\n        <Card delay={100}>\n          <CardTitle icon=\"confirmation_number\">Active Queue</CardTitle>\n          {activeTickets.length === 0 ? (\n            <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant\">No active queue tickets</p>\n          ) : activeTickets.map(t => (\n            <div key={t.id} className=\"flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl mb-2\">\n              <div>\n                <div className=\"font-data-mono font-bold text-primary\">{t.ticket_number}</div>\n                <div className=\"text-xs text-on-surface-variant\">{t.service?.name}</div>\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"text-right\">\n                <StatusBadge status={t.status} />\n                <div className=\"text-xs text-on-surface-variant mt-1\">Position: {t.position}</div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          ))}\n        </Card>\n\n        {/* Upcoming Appointments */}\n        <Card delay={180}>\n          <CardTitle icon=\"event_available\">Upcoming Appointments</CardTitle>\n          {activeAppts.length === 0 ? (\n            <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant\">No upcoming appointments</p>\n          ) : activeAppts.slice(0, 3).map(a => (\n            <div key={a.id} className=\"flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl mb-2\">\n              <div>\n                <div className=\"text-sm font-medium text-on-surface\">{a.service?.name}</div>\n                <div className=\"text-xs text-on-surface-variant\">{a.branch?.name}</div>\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"text-right\">\n                <div className=\"text-sm font-medium text-on-surface\">{a.appointment_date}</div>\n                <div className=\"text-xs text-on-surface-variant\">{a.appointment_time}</div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          ))}\n        </Card>\n\n        {/* Recent Feedback */}\n        <Card delay={240} className=\"lg:col-span-2\">\n          <CardTitle icon=\"forum\">Recent Feedback</CardTitle>\n          {feedbacks.length === 0 ? (\n            <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant\">No feedback submitted yet</p>\n          ) : (\n            <div className=\"overflow-x-auto\">\n              <table className=\"w-full text-sm\">\n                <thead><tr className=\"border-b border-outline-variant/20\">\n                  <th className=\"text-left py-2 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Type</th>\n                  <th className=\"text-left py-2 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Content</th>\n                  <th className=\"text-left py-2 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Sentiment</th>\n                  <th className=\"text-left py-2 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Status</th>\n                </tr></thead>\n                <tbody>\n                  {feedbacks.slice(0, 5).map(f => (\n                    <tr key={f.id} className=\"border-b border-outline-variant/10\">\n                      <td className=\"py-2\"><StatusBadge status={f.type} /></td>\n                      <td className=\"py-2 text-on-surface-variant max-w-xs truncate\">{f.content}</td>\n                      <td className=\"py-2\">\n                        {f.analysis && <span className={`font-medium ${f.analysis.sentiment === 'Positive' ? 'text-tertiary' : f.analysis.sentiment === 'Negative' ? 'text-error' : 'text-primary'}`}>{f.analysis.sentiment}</span>}\n                      </td>\n                      <td className=\"py-2\"><StatusBadge status={f.status} /></td>\n                    </tr>\n                  ))}\n                </tbody>\n              </table>\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </Card>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "Frontend/src/pages/customer/QueuePage.jsx",
  "instructions": "Refine QueuePage with Luma design tokens and scroll reveal animations",
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { api } from '../../services/api';\nimport Reveal from '../../components/ScrollReveal';\nimport { LoadingPage, PageHeader, Card, CardTitle, PrimaryButton, Field, Select } from '../../components/ui/Elements';\nimport { StatusBadge } from '../../components/ui/Elements';\n\nexport default function QueuePage() {\n  const [branches, setBranches] = useState([]);\n  const [services, setServices] = useState([]);\n  const [myTickets, setMyTickets] = useState([]);\n  const [form, setForm] = useState({ branch_id: '', service_id: '' });\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    Promise.all([\n      api.get('/branches/'),\n      api.get('/services/'),\n      api.get('/queues/active')\n    ]).then(([b, s, t]) => {\n      setBranches(b.data);\n      setServices(s.data);\n      setMyTickets(t.data);\n    }).finally(() => setLoading(false));\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleJoin = async () => {\n    if (!form.branch_id || !form.service_id) return;\n    setSubmitting(true);\n    try {\n      await api.post('/queues/', { branch_id: parseInt(form.branch_id), service_id: parseInt(form.service_id) });\n      const res = await api.get('/queues/active');\n      setMyTickets(res.data);\n    } catch (err) {\n      alert(err.message || 'Failed to join queue');\n    } finally {\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  if (loading) return <LoadingPage />;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8\">\n      <PageHeader\n        eyebrow=\"Save your spot from home\"\n        title=\"SmartQueue\"\n        subtitle=\"Join the digital queue and walk in right when it's your turn.\"\n      />\n\n      {/* Active Tickets */}\n      {myTickets.length > 0 && (\n        <Card className=\"mb-6\" direction=\"down\">\n          <CardTitle icon=\"confirmation_number\">Your Active Tickets</CardTitle>\n          <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4\">\n            {myTickets.map((t, i) => (\n              <Reveal key={t.id} delay={i * 90} className=\"bg-surface-container-low rounded-2xl p-5 border border-outline-variant/30\">\n                <div className=\"text-center\">\n                  <div className=\"text-3xl font-data-mono font-bold text-primary mb-2\">{t.ticket_number}</div>\n                  <div className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant mb-1\">{t.service?.name}</div>\n                  <div className=\"text-xs text-on-surface-variant mb-3\">\n                    Position: <span className=\"font-bold text-lg text-on-surface\">{t.position}</span>\n                  </div>\n                  <StatusBadge status={t.status} />\n                  <div className=\"text-xs text-on-surface-variant mt-2\">Est. service: {t.estimated_service_time}</div>\n                </div>\n              </Reveal>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        </Card>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Join Queue */}\n      <Card delay={120}>\n        <CardTitle icon=\"group_add\">Join Queue</CardTitle>\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4\">\n          <Field label=\"Branch\">\n            <Select value={form.branch_id} onChange={e => setForm({...form, branch_id: e.target.value})}>\n              <option value=\"\">Select branch</option>\n              {branches.map(b => <option key={b.id} value={b.id}>{b.name}</option>)}\n            </Select>\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"Service\">\n            <Select value={form.service_id} onChange={e => setForm({...form, service_id: e.target.value})}>\n              <option value=\"\">Select service</option>\n              {services.map(s => <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.name}</option>)}\n            </Select>\n          </Field>\n        </div>\n        <PrimaryButton onClick={handleJoin} disabled={!form.branch_id || !form.service_id || submitting}>\n          {submitting ? 'Joining...' : 'Join Queue'}\n          <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]\">arrow_forward</span>\n        </PrimaryButton>\n      </Card>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "Frontend/src/pages/customer/DocumentsPage.jsx",
  "instructions": "Refine customer DocumentsPage with Luma design tokens and scroll reveal animations",
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';\nimport { api } from '../../services/api';\nimport Reveal from '../../components/ScrollReveal';\nimport { StatusBadge, LoadingPage, PageHeader, Card, CardTitle, PrimaryButton, Field, Select, Input } from '../../components/ui/Elements';\n\nexport default function DocumentsPage() {\n  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState([]);\n  const [services, setServices] = useState([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);\n  const [form, setForm] = useState({ service_id: '', requirement_name: '' });\n  const [selectedFile, setSelectedFile] = useState(null);\n  const fileRef = useRef();\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    Promise.all([\n      api.get('/documents/'),\n      api.get('/services/')\n    ]).then(([d, s]) => {\n      setDocuments(d.data);\n      setServices(s.data);\n    }).finally(() => setLoading(false));\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleUpload = async () => {\n    if (!selectedFile) return;\n    setUploading(true);\n    try {\n      const fd = new FormData();\n      fd.append('file', selectedFile);\n      if (form.service_id) fd.append('service_id', form.service_id);\n      if (form.requirement_name) fd.append('requirement_name', form.requirement_name);\n      await api.upload('/documents/upload', fd);\n      const res = await api.get('/documents/');\n      setDocuments(res.data);\n      setSelectedFile(null);\n      if (fileRef.current) fileRef.current.value = '';\n    } catch (err) {\n      alert(err.message || 'Upload failed');\n    } finally {\n      setUploading(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  if (loading) return <LoadingPage />;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8\">\n      <PageHeader\n        eyebrow=\"Check papers before you leave\"\n        title=\"Documents & TrustVerify\"\n        subtitle=\"Upload your documents and let AI catch mistakes before your visit — never get turned away.\"\n      />\n\n      {/* Upload */}\n      <Card className=\"mb-6\">\n        <CardTitle icon=\"upload_file\">Upload Document</CardTitle>\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4\">\n          <Field label=\"Service\">\n            <Select value={form.service_id} onChange={e => setForm({...form, service_id: e.target.value})}>\n              <option value=\"\">Select service</option>\n              {services.map(s => <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.name}</option>)}\n            </Select>\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"Document Type\">\n            <Input type=\"text\" value={form.requirement_name} onChange={e => setForm({...form, requirement_name: e.target.value})}\n              placeholder=\"e.g. CAC Certificate, Valid ID\" />\n          </Field>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"mb-4\">\n          <Field label=\"File\">\n            <input ref={fileRef} type=\"file\" accept=\".pdf,.jpg,.jpeg,.png\"\n              onChange={e => setSelectedFile(e.target.files[0])}\n              className=\"w-full px-3 py-2 border border-outline-variant/60 rounded-lg text-sm text-on-surface bg-surface-container-lowest file:mr-3 file:py-1.5 file:px-3 file:rounded-md file:border-0 file:bg-primary-container file:text-white file:text-xs file:font-semibold cursor-pointer\" />\n          </Field>\n          <p className=\"text-xs text-outline mt-1 font-data-mono-xs\">Accepted: PDF, JPEG, PNG (max 16MB)</p>\n        </div>\n        <PrimaryButton onClick={handleUpload} disabled={!selectedFile || uploading}>\n          {uploading ? 'Uploading & Verifying...' : 'Upload & Verify'}\n          <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]\">verified_user</span>\n        </PrimaryButton>\n      </Card>\n\n      {/* Documents List */}\n      <Card delay={120}>\n        <CardTitle icon=\"folder_open\">Your Documents</CardTitle>\n        {documents.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant\">No documents uploaded yet</p>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n            {documents.map((doc, i) => (\n              <Reveal key={doc.id} delay={i * 60}\n                className=\"flex items-center justify-between p-4 bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl border border-outline-variant/30\">\n                <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n                  <div className=\"w-10 h-10 bg-primary-fixed rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center\">\n                    <span className=\"text-primary font-data-mono-xs text-xs font-bold\">{doc.mime_type?.includes('pdf') ? 'PDF' : 'IMG'}</span>\n                  </div>\n                  <div>\n                    <div className=\"text-sm font-medium text-on-surface\">{doc.original_filename}</div>\n                    <div className=\"text-xs text-on-surface-variant\">{doc.requirement_name} • {(doc.file_size / 1024).toFixed(0)}KB</div>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n                <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n                  <StatusBadge status={doc.status} />\n                  {doc.verifications?.[0] && (\n                    <div className=\"text-right\">\n                      <div className=\"text-xs text-on-surface-variant\">Confidence</div>\n                      <div className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-tertiary font-data-mono\">{(doc.verifications[0].confidence_score * 100).toFixed(0)}%</div>\n                    </div>\n                  )}\n                </div>\n              </Reveal>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </Card>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "Frontend/src/pages/customer/PaymentsPage.jsx",
  "instructions": "Refine customer PaymentsPage with Luma design tokens and scroll reveal animations",
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { api } from '../../services/api';\nimport Reveal from '../../components/ScrollReveal';\nimport { StatusBadge, LoadingPage, PageHeader, Card, CardTitle, PrimaryButton, Field, Select, Alert } from '../../components/ui/Elements';\n\nexport default function PaymentsPage() {\n  const [payments, setPayments] = useState([]);\n  const [services, setServices] = useState([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [selectedService, setSelectedService] = useState('');\n  const [consentPayment, setConsentPayment] = useState(null);\n  const [paying, setPaying] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState('');\n\n  const refresh = () => api.get('/payments/').then(r => setPayments(r.data));\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    Promise.all([api.get('/payments/'), api.get('/services/')])\n      .then(([p, s]) => { setPayments(p.data); setServices(s.data); })\n      .finally(() => setLoading(false));\n  }, []);\n\n  const handlePay = async () => {\n    const svc = services.find(s => s.id === parseInt(selectedService));\n    if (!svc || svc.fee === 0) return;\n    setError('');\n    setConsentPayment(null);\n    setPaying(true);\n    try {\n      const res = await api.post('/payments/', {\n        service_id: svc.id,\n        amount: svc.fee,\n        narration: `${svc.name} — service fee`\n      });\n      if (res.data.consent_required) {\n        setConsentPayment(res.data.data);\n      }\n      refresh();\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err.message || 'Payment could not be initiated.');\n      refresh();\n    } finally {\n      setPaying(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleVerify = async (pay) => {\n    setError('');\n    try {\n      await api.post(`/payments/${pay.id}/verify`);\n      refresh();\n    } catch (err) {\n      setError(err.message || 'Verification failed.');\n    }\n  };\n\n  if (loading) return <LoadingPage />;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8\">\n      <PageHeader\n        eyebrow=\"Fast & transparent\"\n        title=\"Payments\"\n        subtitle=\"Pay fees directly with instant receipts and no hidden charges.\"\n        action={<span className=\"px-3 py-1 rounded-md bg-primary-fixed text-primary font-data-mono-xs text-xs font-semibold\">ALAT AUTHENTICATOR</span>}\n      />\n\n      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6 mb-6\">\n        {/* Make a Payment */}\n        <Card>\n          <CardTitle icon=\"payments\">Make a Payment</CardTitle>\n          <div className=\"mb-4\">\n            <Field label=\"Select Service\">\n              <Select value={selectedService} onChange={e => setSelectedService(e.target.value)}>\n                <option value=\"\">Select a service to pay for</option>\n                {services.filter(s => s.fee > 0).map(s => (\n                  <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.name} - ₦{s.fee.toLocaleString()}</option>\n                ))}\n              </Select>\n            </Field>\n          </div>\n\n          {selectedService && (\n            <Reveal direction=\"down\" className=\"bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl p-4 mb-4 border border-outline-variant/30\">\n              <div className=\"flex justify-between items-center\">\n                <span className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant\">Service Fee</span>\n                <span className=\"text-xl font-bold text-primary font-data-mono\">\n                  ₦{services.find(s => s.id === parseInt(selectedService))?.fee.toLocaleString()}\n                </span>\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"flex justify-between items-center mt-2\">\n                <span className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant\">Payment Method</span>\n                <span className=\"text-sm font-medium text-on-surface\">ALAT Authenticator</span>\n              </div>\n              <p className=\"text-xs text-on-surface-variant mt-2\">You will be asked to approve the debit in your ALAT app.</p>\n            </Reveal>\n          )}\n\n          {error && <Alert kind=\"error\" className=\"mb-4\">{error}</Alert>}\n\n          <PrimaryButton onClick={handlePay} disabled={!selectedService || paying}>\n            {paying ? 'Processing...' : 'Pay Now'}\n            <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]\">lock</span>\n          </PrimaryButton>\n        </Card>\n\n        {/* Payment status panel */}\n        <div className=\"space-y-6\">\n          {consentPayment && (\n            <Reveal direction=\"left\" className=\"rounded-2xl p-6 bg-primary-fixed/40 border border-primary/30\">\n              <div className=\"flex items-start gap-3\">\n                <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-primary text-2xl\">approval</span>\n                <div>\n                  <h3 className=\"font-semibold text-on-surface\">Approval Required</h3>\n                  <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant mt-1\">\n                    Payment <span className=\"font-data-mono font-semibold\">{consentPayment.transaction_ref}</span> for ₦{consentPayment.amount.toLocaleString()}\n                    {' '}has been initiated. Open your <strong>ALAT app</strong> and approve the debit request to complete the payment.\n                  </p>\n                  <PrimaryButton onClick={() => handleVerify(consentPayment)} className=\"mt-3\">\n                    I've approved — Refresh Status\n                  </PrimaryButton>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </Reveal>\n          )}\n          {!consentPayment && (\n            <Card delay={100} direction=\"right\" className=\"bg-surface-container-low\">\n              <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3 mb-3\">\n                <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-tertiary text-[22px]\">verified_user</span>\n                <h3 className=\"font-semibold text-on-surface\">Bank-Level Protection</h3>\n              </div>\n              <ul className=\"space-y-2 text-sm text-on-surface-variant\">\n                <li className=\"flex items-center gap-2\"><span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-tertiary text-[16px]\">check_circle</span> Every payment is encrypted and receipted instantly.</li>\n                <li className=\"flex items-center gap-2\"><span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-tertiary text-[16px]\">check_circle</span> Approve debits from your own banking app.</li>\n                <li className=\"flex items-center gap-2\"><span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-tertiary text-[16px]\">check_circle</span> Receipts are attached to your visit automatically.</li>\n              </ul>\n            </Card>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* History */}\n      <Card delay={160}>\n        <CardTitle icon=\"receipt_long\">Payment History</CardTitle>\n        {payments.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant\">No payments yet</p>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"overflow-x-auto\">\n            <table className=\"w-full text-sm\">\n              <thead><tr className=\"border-b border-outline-variant/20\">\n                <th className=\"text-left py-2 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Reference</th>\n                <th className=\"text-left py-2 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Amount</th>\n                <th className=\"text-left py-2 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Method</th>\n                <th className=\"text-left py-2 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Status</th>\n                <th className=\"text-left py-2 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Date</th>\n              </tr></thead>\n              <tbody>\n                {payments.map(p => (\n                  <tr key={p.id} className=\"border-b border-outline-variant/10\">\n                    <td className=\"py-2 font-data-mono text-xs text-on-surface\">{p.transaction_ref}</td>\n                    <td className=\"py-2 font-semibold text-on-surface font-data-mono\">₦{p.amount.toLocaleString()}</td>\n                    <td className=\"py-2 text-on-surface-variant\">{p.payment_method}</td>\n                    <td className=\"py-2\"><StatusBadge status={p.status} /></td>\n                    <td className=\"py-2 text-on-surface-variant\">{p.paid_at ? new Date(p.paid_at).toLocaleDateString() : '-'}</td>\n                  </tr>\n                ))}\n              </tbody>\n            </table>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </Card>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

{
  "path": "Frontend/src/pages/customer/FeedbackPage.jsx",
  "instructions": "Refine customer FeedbackPage with Luma design tokens and scroll reveal animations",
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { api } from '../../services/api';\nimport Reveal from '../../components/ScrollReveal';\nimport { LoadingPage, PageHeader, Card, CardTitle, PrimaryButton, Field, Select, Textarea, Alert } from '../../components/ui/Elements';\n\nexport default function FeedbackPage() {\n  const [feedbacks, setFeedbacks] = useState([]);\n  const [branches, setBranches] = useState([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n  const [form, setForm] = useState({ content: '', type: 'FEEDBACK', branch_id: '', rating: 5 });\n  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    Promise.all([\n      api.get('/feedback/my-feedback'),\n      api.get('/branches/')\n    ]).then(([f, b]) => {\n      setFeedbacks(f.data);\n      setBranches(b.data);\n    }).finally(() => setLoading(false));\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleSubmit = async () => {\n    if (!form.content.trim()) return;\n    setSubmitting(true);\n    try {\n      const data = { ...form, branch_id: form.branch_id ? parseInt(form.branch_id) : undefined };\n      const res = await api.post('/feedback/', data);\n      setFeedbacks([res.data, ...feedbacks]);\n      setForm({ content: '', type: 'FEEDBACK', branch_id: '', rating: 5 });\n      setSuccess(true);\n      setTimeout(() => setSuccess(false), 3000);\n    } catch (err) {\n      alert(err.message || 'Failed to submit feedback');\n    } finally {\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  if (loading) return <LoadingPage />;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8\">\n      <PageHeader\n        eyebrow=\"Your voice matters\"\n        title=\"Customer Feedback\"\n        subtitle=\"Tell us how it went — every note helps teams fix what's slow.\"\n      />\n\n      {/* Submit */}\n      <Card className=\"mb-6\">\n        <CardTitle icon=\"rate_review\">Share Your Experience</CardTitle>\n        {success && <Alert kind=\"success\" className=\"mb-4\">Feedback submitted and analyzed successfully!</Alert>}\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4\">\n          <Field label=\"Type\">\n            <Select value={form.type} onChange={e => setForm({...form, type: e.target.value})}>\n              <option value=\"FEEDBACK\">Feedback</option>\n              <option value=\"COMPLAINT\">Complaint</option>\n              <option value=\"QUESTION\">Question</option>\n            </Select>\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"Branch (Optional)\">\n            <Select value={form.branch_id} onChange={e => setForm({...form, branch_id: e.target.value})}>\n              <option value=\"\">Select branch</option>\n              {branches.map(b => <option key={b.id} value={b.id}>{b.name}</option>)}\n            </Select>\n          </Field>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"mb-4\">\n          <Field label=\"Your Experience\">\n            <Textarea value={form.content} onChange={e => setForm({...form, content: e.target.value})}\n              rows={4} placeholder=\"Tell us about your experience...\" />\n          </Field>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"mb-4\">\n          <label className=\"block text-sm font-medium text-on-surface-variant mb-2\">Rating</label>\n          <div className=\"flex gap-2\">\n            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map(n => (\n              <button key={n} onClick={() => setForm({...form, rating: n})} aria-label={`Rate ${n}`}\n                className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-lg font-semibold text-sm transition-all ${form.rating >= n ? 'bg-primary-container text-white shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(0,82,255,0.25)]' : 'bg-surface-container text-outline hover:bg-surface-container-high'}`}>\n                {n}\n              </button>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <PrimaryButton onClick={handleSubmit} disabled={!form.content.trim() || submitting}>\n          {submitting ? 'Submitting...' : 'Submit Feedback'}\n          <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]\">send</span>\n        </PrimaryButton>\n      </Card>\n\n      {/* History */}\n      <Card delay={120}>\n        <CardTitle icon=\"history\">Your Feedback History</CardTitle>\n        {feedbacks.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant\">No feedback submitted yet</p>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n            {feedbacks.map((f, i) => (\n              <Reveal key={f.id} delay={i * 60} className=\"p-4 bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl border border-outline-variant/30\">\n                <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between mb-2\">\n                  <span className={`inline-block px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium\n                    ${f.type === 'COMPLAINT' ? 'bg-error-container text-on-error-container' : f.type === 'QUESTION' ? 'bg-primary-fixed text-primary' : 'bg-tertiary-fixed text-tertiary'}`}>\n                    {f.type}\n                  </span>\n                  <span className=\"text-xs text-on-surface-variant font-data-mono-xs\">{new Date(f.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>\n                </div>\n                <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface mb-2\">{f.content}</p>\n                {f.analysis && (\n                  <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-4 text-xs text-on-surface-variant\">\n                    <span>Sentiment: <span className={`font-semibold ${f.analysis.sentiment === 'Positive' ? 'text-tertiary' : f.analysis.sentiment === 'Negative' ? 'text-error' : 'text-primary'}`}>{f.analysis.sentiment}</span></span>\n                    <span>Topic: {f.analysis.topic}</span>\n                    <span>Priority: {f.analysis.priority}</span>\n                  </div>\n                )}\n              </Reveal>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </Card>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "Frontend/src/pages/branch/BranchDashboard.jsx",
  "instructions": "Refine BranchDashboard with Luma design tokens and scroll reveal animations",
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { api } from '../../services/api';\nimport { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';\nimport Reveal from '../../components/ScrollReveal';\nimport { StatusBadge, LoadingPage, Card, CardTitle } from '../../components/ui/Elements';\n\nexport default function BranchDashboard() {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  const [queue, setQueue] = useState({ waiting: [], called: [], in_service: [], total_waiting: 0 });\n  const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n\n  const branchId = user?.branch_id;\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!branchId) { setLoading(false); return; }\n    Promise.all([\n      api.get(`/branches/${branchId}/queue`),\n      api.get(`/appointments/branch/${branchId}`)\n    ]).then(([q, a]) => {\n      setQueue(q.data);\n      setAppointments(a.data);\n    }).finally(() => setLoading(false));\n  }, [branchId]);\n\n  const callNext = async (ticketId) => {\n    await api.post(`/queues/${ticketId}/call`);\n    const res = await api.get(`/branches/${branchId}/queue`);\n    setQueue(res.data);\n  };\n\n  const serveTicket = async (ticketId) => {\n    await api.post(`/queues/${ticketId}/serve`);\n    const res = await api.get(`/branches/${branchId}/queue`);\n    setQueue(res.data);\n  };\n\n  const completeTicket = async (ticketId) => {\n    await api.post(`/queues/${ticketId}/complete`);\n    const res = await api.get(`/branches/${branchId}/queue`);\n    setQueue(res.data);\n  };\n\n  if (loading) return <LoadingPage />;\n\n  const stats = [\n    { label: 'Waiting', value: queue.total_waiting, color: 'text-primary', icon: 'hourglass_top' },\n    { label: 'Called', value: queue.called?.length || 0, color: 'text-primary-container', icon: 'campaign' },\n    { label: 'In Service', value: queue.in_service?.length || 0, color: 'text-tertiary', icon: 'support_agent' },\n    { label: \"Today's Appointments\", value: appointments.filter(a => a.status === 'SCHEDULED').length, color: 'text-on-surface', icon: 'event_available' },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8\">\n      <Reveal direction=\"down\" className=\"mb-8 flex items-center justify-between gap-4\">\n        <div>\n          <span className=\"font-data-mono-xs text-xs uppercase text-primary font-semibold tracking-wider\">Branch operations</span>\n          <h1 className=\"font-headline-md text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold text-on-surface tracking-tight\">Branch Dashboard</h1>\n          <p className=\"text-on-surface-variant text-sm\">Manage queue, appointments, and customer service</p>\n        </div>\n        <span className=\"hidden sm:inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-tertiary-fixed/40 text-tertiary font-data-mono-xs text-xs font-semibold\">\n          <span className=\"w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-tertiary-container animate-pulse\"></span> Live queue active\n        </span>\n      </Reveal>\n\n      {/* Queue Summary */}\n      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-8\">\n        {stats.map((s, i) => (\n          <Reveal key={s.label} delay={i * 80} className=\"bg-surface-container-lowest border border-outline-variant/40 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm\">\n            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between mb-1\">\n              <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-outline-variant text-[20px]\">{s.icon}</span>\n              <div className={`text-3xl font-bold ${s.color} font-data-mono`}>{s.value}</div>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant\">{s.label}</div>\n          </Reveal>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6\">\n        {/* Queue */}\n        <Card delay={100}>\n          <CardTitle icon=\"confirmation_number\">Live Queue</CardTitle>\n          {queue.waiting?.length === 0 && queue.called?.length === 0 && queue.in_service?.length === 0 ? (\n            <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant\">No customers in queue</p>\n          ) : (\n            <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n              {[...(queue.called || []), ...(queue.in_service || []), ...(queue.waiting || [])].map((t, i) => (\n                <Reveal key={t.id} delay={Math.min(i * 50, 250)}\n                  className=\"flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl border border-outline-variant/30\">\n                  <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n                    <div className=\"font-data-mono font-bold text-primary w-20\">{t.ticket_number}</div>\n                    <div>\n                      <div className=\"text-sm font-medium text-on-surface\">{t.service?.name}</div>\n                      <div className=\"text-xs text-on-surface-variant\">Pos: {t.position} • Est: {t.estimated_service_time}</div>\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n                  <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                    <StatusBadge status={t.status} />\n                    {t.status === 'WAITING' && (\n                      <button onClick={() => callNext(t.id)} className=\"text-xs bg-primary-fixed text-primary px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg font-medium hover:bg-primary hover:text-white transition-colors\">Call</button>\n                    )}\n                    {t.status === 'CALLED' && (\n                      <button onClick={() => serveTicket(t.id)} className=\"text-xs bg-primary-fixed text-primary px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg font-medium hover:bg-primary hover:text-white transition-colors\">Serve</button>\n                    )}\n                    {t.status === 'IN_SERVICE' && (\n                      <button onClick={() => completeTicket(t.id)} className=\"text-xs bg-tertiary-fixed text-tertiary px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg font-medium hover:bg-tertiary hover:text-white transition-colors\">Complete</button>\n                    )}\n                  </div>\n                </Reveal>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </Card>\n\n        {/* Appointments */}\n        <Card delay={180}>\n          <CardTitle icon=\"event_note\">Appointments</CardTitle>\n          {appointments.length === 0 ? (\n            <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant\">No appointments</p>\n          ) : (\n            <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n              {appointments.slice(0, 8).map((a, i) => (\n                <Reveal key={a.id} delay={Math.min(i * 50, 250)}\n                  className=\"flex items-center justify-between p-3 bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl border border-outline-variant/30\">\n                  <div>\n                    <div className=\"text-sm font-medium text-on-surface\">{a.service?.name}</div>\n                    <div className=\"text-xs text-on-surface-variant\">{a.appointment_date} at {a.appointment_time}</div>\n                  </div>\n                  <StatusBadge status={a.status} />\n                </Reveal>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </Card>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "Frontend/src/pages/branch/BranchAppointmentsPage.jsx",
  "instructions": "Refine BranchAppointmentsPage with Luma design tokens and scroll reveal animations",
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { api } from '../../services/api';\nimport { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';\nimport Reveal from '../../components/ScrollReveal';\nimport { StatusBadge, LoadingPage, PageHeader } from '../../components/ui/Elements';\n\nexport default function BranchAppointmentsPage() {\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('');\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!user?.branch_id) { setLoading(false); return; }\n    const params = filter ? `?status=${filter}` : '';\n    api.get(`/appointments/branch/${user.branch_id}${params}`).then(res => setAppointments(res.data)).finally(() => setLoading(false));\n  }, [user, filter]);\n\n  if (loading) return <LoadingPage />;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8\">\n      <PageHeader\n        eyebrow=\"Scheduled visits\"\n        title=\"Branch Appointments\"\n        subtitle=\"Every booked customer, with their papers pre-checked before arrival.\"\n      />\n\n      <Reveal direction=\"down\" delay={80} className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-6\">\n        {['', 'SCHEDULED', 'CONFIRMED', 'COMPLETED', 'CANCELLED'].map(f => (\n          <button key={f} onClick={() => setFilter(f)}\n            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition-all ${filter === f ? 'bg-primary-container text-white shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(0,82,255,0.25)]' : 'bg-surface-container-lowest border border-outline-variant/40 text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-container'}`}>\n            {f ? f.replace(/_/g, ' ').toLowerCase().replace(/\\b\\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase()) : 'All'}\n          </button>\n        ))}\n      </Reveal>\n\n      <Reveal delay={140} className=\"bg-surface-container-lowest border border-outline-variant/40 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm\">\n        {appointments.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant text-center py-10\">No appointments found</p>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"overflow-x-auto\">\n            <table className=\"w-full text-sm\">\n              <thead className=\"bg-surface-container-low\">\n                <tr>\n                  <th className=\"text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Customer</th>\n                  <th className=\"text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Service</th>\n                  <th className=\"text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Date</th>\n                  <th className=\"text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Time</th>\n                  <th className=\"text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Status</th>\n                </tr>\n              </thead>\n              <tbody>\n                {appointments.map((a, i) => (\n                  <tr key={a.id} className=\"border-t border-outline-variant/15 hover:bg-surface-container-low/60 transition-colors\">\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 text-on-surface\">{a.customer_id ? `Customer #${a.customer_id}` : '-'}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 text-on-surface-variant\">{a.service?.name || '-'}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 text-on-surface-variant font-data-mono-xs\">{a.appointment_date}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 text-on-surface-variant font-data-mono-xs\">{a.appointment_time}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3\"><StatusBadge status={a.status} /></td>\n                  </tr>\n                ))}\n              </tbody>\n            </table>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </Reveal>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "Frontend/src/pages/branch/BranchDocumentsPage.jsx",
  "instructions": "Refine BranchDocumentsPage with Luma design tokens and scroll reveal animations",
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { api } from '../../services/api';\nimport Reveal from '../../components/ScrollReveal';\nimport { StatusBadge, LoadingPage, PageHeader } from '../../components/ui/Elements';\n\nexport default function BranchDocumentsPage() {\n  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    api.get('/documents/').then(res => setDocuments(res.data)).finally(() => setLoading(false));\n  }, []);\n\n  if (loading) return <LoadingPage />;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8\">\n      <PageHeader\n        eyebrow=\"Pre-verified paperwork\"\n        title=\"Document Review\"\n        subtitle=\"AI-checked documents submitted by customers before their visits.\"\n      />\n\n      <Reveal delay={100} className=\"bg-surface-container-lowest border border-outline-variant/40 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm\">\n        {documents.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant text-center py-10\">No documents to review</p>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"overflow-x-auto\">\n            <table className=\"w-full text-sm\">\n              <thead className=\"bg-surface-container-low\">\n                <tr>\n                  <th className=\"text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">File</th>\n                  <th className=\"text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Type</th>\n                  <th className=\"text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Status</th>\n                  <th className=\"text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Verification</th>\n                  <th className=\"text-left px-4 py-3 font-medium text-on-surface-variant\">Confidence</th>\n                </tr>\n              </thead>\n              <tbody>\n                {documents.map(doc => (\n                  <tr key={doc.id} className=\"border-t border-outline-variant/15 hover:bg-surface-container-low/60 transition-colors\">\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 text-on-surface font-medium\">{doc.original_filename}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 text-on-surface-variant\">{doc.requirement_name || '-'}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3\"><StatusBadge status={doc.status} /></td>\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">\n                      {doc.verifications?.[0] ? (\n                        <StatusBadge status={doc.verifications[0].overall_status} />\n                      ) : <span className=\"text-outline-variant\">-</span>}\n                    </td>\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 text-on-surface-variant font-data-mono\">\n                      {doc.verifications?.[0] ? `${(doc.verifications[0].confidence_score * 100).toFixed(0)}%` : '-'}\n                    </td>\n                  </tr>\n                ))}\n              </tbody>\n            </table>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </Reveal>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "Frontend/src/pages/branch/BranchConnectPage.jsx",
  "instructions": "Refine BranchConnectPage with Luma design tokens and scroll reveal animations",
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { api } from '../../services/api';\nimport Reveal from '../../components/ScrollReveal';\nimport { LoadingPage, PageHeader, Card, CardTitle, PrimaryButton, Field, Input, Select, Textarea } from '../../components/ui/Elements';\n\nexport default function BranchConnectPage() {\n  const [posts, setPosts] = useState([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('');\n  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');\n  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);\n  const [form, setForm] = useState({\n    branch_id: '', title: '', content: '', post_type: 'SOLUTION', category: '',\n    solution: { problem: '', solution: '', result: '' }\n  });\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const params = new URLSearchParams();\n    if (filter) params.set('type', filter);\n    if (search) params.set('search', search);\n    api.get(`/branchconnect/posts?${params}`).then(res => setPosts(res.data)).finally(() => setLoading(false));\n  }, [filter, search]);\n\n  const handleCreate = async () => {\n    try {\n      const data = { ...form, branch_id: parseInt(form.branch_id) || 1 };\n      if (form.post_type !== 'SOLUTION') delete data.solution;\n      await api.post('/branchconnect/posts', data);\n      const res = await api.get('/branchconnect/posts');\n      setPosts(res.data);\n      setShowForm(false);\n      setForm({ branch_id: '', title: '', content: '', post_type: 'SOLUTION', category: '', solution: { problem: '', solution: '', result: '' } });\n    } catch (err) {\n      alert(err.message || 'Failed to create post');\n    }\n  };\n\n  const markUseful = async (postId) => {\n    await api.post(`/branchconnect/posts/${postId}/useful`);\n    setPosts(posts.map(p => p.id === postId ? { ...p, useful_count: p.useful_count + 1, is_useful: true } : p));\n  };\n\n  if (loading) return <LoadingPage />;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8\">\n      <PageHeader\n        eyebrow=\"Shared best practices\"\n        title=\"BranchConnect\"\n        subtitle=\"When one branch learns a faster way to solve a problem, every other branch learns it too.\"\n        action={\n          <PrimaryButton onClick={() => setShowForm(!showForm)}>\n            {showForm ? 'Cancel' : 'Share Solution'}\n            <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]\">{showForm ? 'close' : 'add_circle'}</span>\n          </PrimaryButton>\n        }\n      />\n\n      {/* Filters */}\n      <Reveal direction=\"down\" delay={80} className=\"flex gap-3 mb-6\">\n        <Input type=\"text\" placeholder=\"Search solutions...\" value={search} onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)} />\n        <Select value={filter} onChange={e => setFilter(e.target.value)} className=\"max-w-[180px]\">\n          <option value=\"\">All Types</option>\n          <option value=\"SOLUTION\">Solutions</option>\n          <option value=\"LESSON_LEARNED\">Lessons Learned</option>\n          <option value=\"TREND\">Trends</option>\n        </Select>\n      </Reveal>\n\n      {/* Create Form */}\n      {showForm && (\n        <Reveal direction=\"down\" className=\"mb-6\">\n          <Card reveal={false}>\n            <CardTitle icon=\"lightbulb\">Share a Solution</CardTitle>\n            <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4\">\n              <Field label=\"Type\">\n                <Select value={form.post_type} onChange={e => setForm({...form, post_type: e.target.value})}>\n                  <option value=\"SOLUTION\">Solution</option>\n                  <option value=\"LESSON_LEARNED\">Lesson Learned</option>\n                  <option value=\"TREND\">Customer Trend</option>\n                </Select>\n              </Field>\n              <Field label=\"Category\">\n                <Input type=\"text\" value={form.category} onChange={e => setForm({...form, category: e.target.value})}\n                  placeholder=\"e.g. Queue Management\" />\n              </Field>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"mb-4\">\n              <Field label=\"Title\">\n                <Input type=\"text\" value={form.title} onChange={e => setForm({...form, title: e.target.value})} />\n              </Field>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"mb-4\">\n              <Field label=\"Content\">\n                <Textarea value={form.content} onChange={e => setForm({...form, content: e.target.value})} rows={3} />\n              </Field>\n            </div>\n            {form.post_type === 'SOLUTION' && (\n              <div className=\"bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl p-4 mb-4 space-y-3 border border-outline-variant/30\">\n                <Field label=\"Problem\">\n                  <Input type=\"text\" value={form.solution.problem} onChange={e => setForm({...form, solution: {...form.solution, problem: e.target.value}})} />\n                </Field>\n                <Field label=\"Solution\">\n                  <Input type=\"text\" value={form.solution.solution} onChange={e => setForm({...form, solution: {...form.solution, solution: e.target.value}})} />\n                </Field>\n                <Field label=\"Result\">\n                  <Input type=\"text\" value={form.solution.result} onChange={e => setForm({...form, solution: {...form.solution, result: e.target.value}})} />\n                </Field>\n              </div>\n            )}\n            <PrimaryButton onClick={handleCreate}>\n              Publish\n              <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]\">send</span>\n            </PrimaryButton>\n          </Card>\n        </Reveal>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Posts */}\n      <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n        {posts.length === 0 ? (\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant text-center py-10\">No posts found</p>\n        ) : posts.map((post, i) => (\n          <Reveal key={post.id} delay={Math.min(i * 70, 350)}\n            className=\"bg-surface-container-lowest border border-outline-variant/40 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm\">\n            <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 mb-2\">\n              <span className={`text-xs font-medium px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${post.post_type === 'SOLUTION' ? 'bg-tertiary-fixed text-tertiary' : post.post_type === 'LESSON_LEARNED' ? 'bg-primary-fixed text-primary' : 'bg-surface-container-highest text-on-surface-variant'}`}>\n                {post.post_type?.replace(/_/g, ' ')}\n              </span>\n              {post.category && <span className=\"text-xs text-on-surface-variant\">{post.category}</span>}\n            </div>\n            <h3 className=\"font-semibold text-on-surface mb-1\">{post.title}</h3>\n            <p className=\"text-sm text-on-surface-variant mb-3\">{post.content}</p>\n            {post.solutions?.[0] && (\n              <div className=\"bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl p-4 mb-3 space-y-2 border border-outline-variant/30\">\n                <div><span className=\"text-xs font-semibold text-primary\">Problem:</span> <span className=\"text-sm text-on-surface\">{post.solutions[0].problem}</span></div>\n                <div><span className=\"text-xs font-semibold text-tertiary\">Solution:</span> <span className=\"text-sm text-on-surface\">{post.solutions[0].solution}</span></div>\n                {post.solutions[0].result && <div><span className=\"text-xs font-semibold text-primary-container\">Result:</span> <span className=\"text-sm text-on-surface\">{post.solutions[0].result}</span></div>}\n                <div className=\"text-xs text-outline font-data-mono-xs\">Used by {post.solutions[0].adopted_by_count} branches</div>\n              </div>\n            )}\n            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n              <div className=\"text-xs text-on-surface-variant\">\n                {post.author?.first_name} {post.author?.last_name} • {post.branch?.name}\n              </div>\n              <button onClick={() => markUseful(post.id)} disabled={post.is_useful}\n                className={`text-xs px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-medium transition-colors ${post.is_useful ? 'bg-tertiary-fixed text-tertiary' : 'bg-surface-container text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-container-high'}`}>\n                Useful ({post.useful_count})\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </Reveal>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}
